Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any composites module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 193. The projective resolution has length at most 740 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to composites structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 193/740 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a composites algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 752. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 842/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 752 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the composites structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 842. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
